# favorite repeater
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_csort_events.110
	title = cosmo_csort_events.110.t
	desc = cosmo_csort_events.110.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
		any_owned_province = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_1
			NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_repeat
			}
		}
		any_subject_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_1
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_repeat
				}
			}			
		}
		}
		}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {		
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_1
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_repeat
				}					
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_favorite_repeat
				duration = 240  
				hidden = yes
			}				
		}		
	}
	}		
		
	option = { #Advisors
		name = cosmo_csort_events.110.12	
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_csort_events.11
			days = 5
			random = 55				
			}						
	}	
						
}

# Consort Visit sort
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_csort_events.11
	title = cosmo_csort_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_csort_events.11.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
		set_ruler_flag = set_cosmo_favorite_1
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}

# Consort Visit sort
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_csort_events.120
	title = cosmo_csort_events.120.t
	desc = cosmo_csort_events.120.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
		any_owned_province = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_2
			NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_repeat
			}
		}
		any_subject_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_2
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_repeat
				}
			}			
		}
		}
		}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {		
			random_province = {

				}				
			}

		}

	title = cosmo_csort_events.130.t
	desc = cosmo_csort_events.130.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
		any_owned_province = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_3
			NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_repeat
			}
		}
		any_subject_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_3
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_repeat
				}
			}			
		}
		}
		}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {		
			random_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_3
					NOT = {
						has_province_modifier = cosmo_favorite_repeat
					}					
				}
				add_province_modifier = { 
					name = cosmo_favorite_repeat
					duration = 240  
					hidden = yes
				}				
			}			
		}
		}		
		
	option = { #Advisors
		name = cosmo_csort_events.130.12
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_csort_events.13
			days = 5
			random = 55				
			}						
	}

# no favorite repeater
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_csort_events.140
	title = cosmo_csort_events.140.t
	desc = cosmo_csort_events.140.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		has_country_modifier = cosmo_no_favorites
		NOT = {
			has_country_modifier = cosmo_no_favorites_repeater
		}
		}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {		
		add_country_modifier = {
			name = cosmo_no_favorites_repeater
			duration = 120  
			hidden = yes			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
				any_owned_province = {
					cosmo_has_concubine_trigger = yes
					NOT = {
						has_province_flag = cosmo_visited_concubine
					}
				}
				any_subject_country = {
					any_owned_province = {
						cosmo_has_concubine_trigger = yes
						NOT = {
							has_province_flag = cosmo_visited_concubine
						}
					}			
				}
				}
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					cosmo_has_concubine_trigger = yes				
				}
				clr_province_flag = cosmo_visited_concubine				
			}			
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				cosmo_has_concubine_trigger = yes	
				NOT = {
						has_province_flag = cosmo_visited_concubine
					}								
			}
				add_province_modifier = { 
					name = cosmo_random_concubine_province
					duration = -1  
					hidden = yes
				}			
		}										
		}
		}		
		
	option = { #Advisors
		name = cosmo_csort_events.140.12	
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = cosmo_no_reapter_start
				}
			}
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_csort_events.14
				days = 5
				random = 55				
			}			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_no_reapter_start
			}
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_csort_events.14			
			}			
		}						
	}	
						
}

	id = cosmo_csort_events.2
	title = cosmo_csort_events.2.t
	desc = cosmo_csort_events.2.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_subject_country = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = new_mod_subjecttype
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = is_cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_I
						}

							who = ROOT
							modifier = is_cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_II
						}
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = is_cosmo_african_personal_union_I
						}
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = is_cosmo_african_personal_union_II
						}
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = is_cosmo_sa_personal_union_II
						}
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = is_cosmo_sa_personal_union_I
						}

							who = ROOT
							modifier = is_cosmo_na_personal_union_I
						}
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = is_cosmo_na_personal_union_II
						}												
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = is_cosmo_asian_personal_union_I

						}													
				}
			NOT = {
				is_subject_of_type = vassal

		}

		}

				limit = {
			 		NOT = {
			 		any_country = {
			 			primary_culture = event_target:consortprovince
			 		}
			 		}
			 	}
			 		set_saved_name = {
			 			key = consort_name
			 			type = advisor
						female = yes
			 			scope = ROOT
			 		}
			 }				
			 if = {
			 	limit = {
			 		any_country = {
			 			primary_culture = event_target:consortprovince
			 		}
			 	}
			 	random_country = {
			 		limit = {
			 			primary_culture = event_target:consortprovince
			 		}
					ROOT = {
			 		set_saved_name = {
			 			key = consort_name
			 			type = advisor
						female = yes
			 			scope = PREV
			 		}
					}

# Consort Visit sort
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_csort_events.6
	title = cosmo_csort_events.6.t
	desc = cosmo_csort_events.6.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		has_country_flag = remove_consort_marriage_privilege
		}
		
		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		clr_country_flag = remove_consort_marriage_privilege		
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				remove_consort = yes
			}
			50 = {
				add_legitimacy = -20
				remove_consort = yes
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					consort_adm = 2
				}
				kill_ruler = yes
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					always = no
					consort_dip = 2
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_province = {
							country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
							owner = {
								consort_culture = PREV 
							}
							OR = {
								is_island = yes
								AND = {
									has_port = yes
									NOT = {
										any_neighbor_province = {
											country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT							
										}
									}									
								}
							}
						}
					}
					random_province = {
						limit = {
							country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
							owner = {
								consort_culture = PREV 
							}
							OR = {
								is_island = yes
								AND = {
									has_port = yes
									NOT = {
										any_neighbor_province = {
											country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT							
										}
									}									
								}
							}							
						}
						cede_province = PIR
					}
				}
				remove_consort = yes
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					consort_mil = 2
				}
				random_owned_province = {
					limit = {
						is_in_capital_area = yes
						NOT = {
							is_capital_of = ROOT
						}
					}
					spawn_rebels = {
						use_consort_as_leader = yes
						type = pretender_rebels
						size = 1
					}					
				}
			}												
		}		
		remove_estate_privilege = estate_nobleaffairs_remove_consort
		}
		}		
		
	
	option = { 
		name = cosmo_csort_events.6.a	
		clr_country_flag = remove_consort_marriage_privilege		
		remove_estate_privilege = estate_nobleaffairs_remove_consort
		}						
}
